"The number is in charge of overseeing all income and costs of public administration and social security institutions under the central management budget and to ensure the account and procedures of the responsible on behalf of the Grand National Assembly of Turkey and to carry out the audits, audits, and decisions given by law." It's called. Furthermore, if added to social security institutions were left aside, the same sentence was included in Article 127 of the 1961 Constitution. Nor was there any exception in that decree. In this regard, the 3rd Law of 5018. The state administration under the central administration (b) refers to public administrations (I), (II) and (III). It is referred to as "I" (I) and "I" (II). So there is no doubt that there is a public administration within the central management budget of the TBM. As such, the Constitution must be audited by the Court in accordance with Article 160 of the Constitution. Unfortunately, according to the implementation before Law 5018, TBM's accounts are not audited by the Court, but are satisfied with being examined by the National Assembly's Commission for Review of Accounts in accordance with the National Assembly's Institution. The grounds for this were Article 127 (27) for which the Court had no right to study the Court's expenditures, the presidential office, the Court and the Court's expenditures of Bookkeeping Law 1050. However, the Court has both control and jurisdiction. As it is known, the audits carried out by the Court have first been reviewed by the auditors to determine the costs against the legislation, and then the defences of those responsible are taken and filed with the report. These reports have been filed by the Court's offices in the case of an account court and have been tried and issued a decree. These are the terms of compensation for non-lawyer payments. This is the way to collect payments from the budget that are against the legislation. It has to be known that the Parliament can oversee all public spending through the Court, and there is no doubt that it must oversee its own budget, but it is not in accordance with the dignity or accountability of the High Assembly. State of emergency management MADDE 119- The President may declare an extraordinary state of state in case of war, war, war, mobilisation, riots, a strong and action against the country or the Republic, the spread of acts of violence that endangers the country's undistribution from within or outside, constitutional order or widespread acts of violence aimed at eliminating fundamental rights and freedoms, in the event of a severe disaster or severe economic crisis. It is published in the official newspaper on the day of the State of Emergency decision and is submitted to the approval of the Grand National Assembly of Turkey on the same day. If Turkey is on holiday, the Grand National Assembly of Turkey is called to a meeting immediately; Parliament can shorten, extend, or remove the state of emergency if necessary. At the request of the president, the Turkish Grand National Assembly can extend the deadline for four months at a time. In cases of war, that is not a four-month period. In extraordinary cases, the money for citizens is regulated by law in accordance with the principles of goods and labour obligations and the principles of Article 15 on which basic rights and freedoms will be limited or temporarily halted, which provisions will be carried out and how procedures will be carried out. In extraordinary cases, the President may issue a presidential decree on matters which are essential to the state of emergency, without subject to restrictions stated in the second sentence of Article 104's 17th joke. These decrees under the law are published in the official newspaper and submitted to Parliament's approval on the same day. The presidential decrees issued during the state of emergency are held within three months in Turkey's Grand National Assembly, excluding the failure of the Grand National Assembly of Turkey for war and murky reasons. Otherwise, the presidential decree, which has been issued in extraordinary situations, will automatically be lifted. There are seven motions on the 12th article, five of which are the same property, and the first five proposals I'll make are the same. The budget law shall have no provisions other than budgetary provisions. The President submits the budget law proposal to the Grand National Assembly of Turkey at least seventy-five days before fiscal Christmas. The budget offer is discussed at the Budget Commission. The text the commission will accept within fifty-five days will be discussed in the General Assembly, and it will be decided by fiscal New Year's Eve. If the budget law is not implemented during the duration, the temporary budget law will be passed. If the temporary budget law is not adopted, the budget of the previous year will be applied according to the rate of revaluement until the new budget law is adopted. Members of the Grand National Assembly of Turkey explain their views on public administration budgets at each time the budget is discussed, they cannot offer extenuating or reducing revenues. The General Assembly has proposed amendments to public administration budgets, which are also read and voted on without discussion. It is imperative that financial resources be provided to meet the envisioned costs, as well as to offer changes in the annual budget, which bring financial burden to the current budgets of years and years to follow. The Central Government draft law proposal is submitted by the President to the Grand National Assembly of Turkey six months later, starting at the end of its fiscal year.
